# PARQUET-PARTITION-DISCOVERY-1 — Step 0 domain table and design

- **Oracle:** live Spark 4.1.2 + Iceberg 1.11.0 (`local[2]`, Java 17).
- **Main:** `514281bd4` (`pkg-d1merged-ea94eedc`, tree-equal to `514281bd4`).
- **Head:** rebuilt release package at branch `HEAD`.
- **Total measured cells:** 719

## Design (Step 0.6)

1. **Narrow seat fix (`crates/repark-core/src/spark_nullable.rs` + sibling `crates/repark-core/src/spark_nullable/partition.rs`):**
   - Keep `crates/repark-core/src/partition_discovery.rs` (`discover_partitions`), `text_scan.rs`, `orc_scan.rs`, CSV, JSON, and Iceberg untouched so zero control cells move.
   - Expose `partition_discovery` primitives (`HIVE_DEFAULT_PARTITION`, `unescape_partition_value`, `conflicting_names_message`, `parse_timestamp_micros_zone`) as `pub(crate)` and reuse them in `spark_nullable/partition.rs`.
   - For directory / S3-prefix Parquet reads (`table_url.is_collection()` with no glob), list objects via the resolved `ObjectStore`, skip any hidden segment (`.` or `_` prefix in any relative directory or file name, covering `_SUCCESS`, `_temporary/`, `.crc`, `.hidden.parquet`, `p=1/.dot.parquet`, `p=1/_under.parquet`), filter `.parquet` files with `size > 0`, apply `keep_partitioned_only` (when any file lives under a `k=v` directory, root files are ignored), and run Spark-exact partition discovery over relative segments below `table_url.prefix()`:
     - Leaf reads (`root/p=1`) and root paths containing `=` (`.../tag=foo`) have zero relative `k=v` directories, so they discover no partition columns and stay on the existing `ListingTable` path.
     - Empty partition value (`p=/`) refuses loudly (matching Spark 4.1.2 refusal).
     - Conflicting column names or mixed depths refuse with `[CONFLICTING_PARTITION_COLUMN_NAMES] ... SQLSTATE: KD009` (case-insensitive under `spark.sql.caseSensitive=false`, case-sensitive under `true`); Python facade attaches `errorClass="CONFLICTING_PARTITION_COLUMN_NAMES"` and `sqlState="KD009"`.
     - `__HIVE_DEFAULT_PARTITION__` maps to NULL; an all-NULL column infers `DataType::Null` (`void`), even with `partitionColumnTypeInference.enabled=false`.
     - Type inference (`spark.sql.sources.partitionColumnTypeInference.enabled`, default `true`, wired as a session `ConfigExtension` across builder, `spark.conf.set`/`unset`, and SQL `SET`/`RESET`): when `false`, non-all-null columns infer `Utf8`; when `true`, per-value Spark ladder (`Int32` -> `Int64` -> `Decimal128(p, 0)` for >i64 integer up to 38 digits -> `Float64` -> `Date32` -> `Timestamp(us, session_zone)` for `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss` and single-digit fractional `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.S` -> `Utf8`) unified with Spark conflict resolution (`Decimal` + `Float64` -> `Utf8`, `Int` + `Decimal` -> `Decimal`, `Int` + `Float64` -> `Float64`, etc.).
     - Data-file + partition-directory column overlap: when a file column matches a partition column (case-insensitively when `caseSensitive=false`, exact when `true`), the file column is dropped from the scan schema and the partition column (name, inferred type, and directory value) replaces it at the original file-column position via logical `Projection`.
   - Execute partitioned Parquet reads through a `PartitionedParquetProvider` (`TableProvider`) backed by DataFusion's `FileScanConfigBuilder` + `FloatBoundsInexactFormat` + `TableSchema::new(file_schema, partition_fields)` with `PartitionedFile.partition_values` populated directly from the discovered `ScalarValue`s.
   - **Partition pruning:** `supports_filters_pushdown` returns `Inexact`, and `scan()` evaluates partition-only filters against each `PartitionedFile`'s `partition_values` (conservatively keeping a file on any evaluation error so Spark ANSI errors never disappear), pruning non-matching files before building `FileScanConfig` (so `where("p = 2")` scans 1 file instead of 2, matching Spark).
   - **Plan introspection:** teach `plan_introspect.rs` (`plan_reads_s3_prefix` and `write_table_source`) to inspect `PartitionedParquetProvider`'s `table_url` alongside `ListingTable`.
2. **Explicitly out of narrow seat (remain on card's "still unlike Spark" list):**
   - CSV (`csv_header`, `csv_noheader`) and JSON — separate seats (`read_csv_path`, `read_json`, `_apply_reader_schema_semantics`).
   - `basePath`, `recursiveFileLookup`, `mergeSchema` — remain refused loud on Parquet (`_UNSUPPORTED_SEMANTIC_READER_OPTIONS`); no guard relaxed.
   - Glob paths (`p=*`, `p=*/*.parquet`) and multi-path varargs (`read.parquet(p1, p2)`) on Parquet.
   - `input_file_name()` on plain-path reads (disclosed R-FN-BATCH4).
